# de-partners-and-part-year: figure and rule ledger

Guide: [de-partners-and-part-year](../../skills/germany/de-partners-and-part-year.md). Accepted Guide SHA-256: `0839172b17872bebeed72749eb57e3353d83c4a6132dbb8e02f04ec0ceefb245`.
Repackaged from the completed [independent country review](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/blob/bceaf70083a8c8573ba8558a339ab11b1b4095bf/ledgers/germany-2026-evidence/independent-review/report.md) at `bceaf70083a8c8573ba8558a339ab11b1b4095bf`. The Guide bytes, all current ledger claims and all original-topic destinations are preserved. This is a packaging change; it does not claim a new legal review or accountant attestation.

Full source captures, earlier versions, scripts and review chains remain in the original archive; none is added by this replacement. Source excerpts below retain their actual read dates. Short excerpts identify the previously reviewed operative unit; they do not replace its complete conditions.

| 001 | Germany: partners' profit shares and the year of moving to or from Germany, tax year 2026; LF18–18 | This skill covers two gaps the other Germany skills leave open. Part 1 is the partner in a partnership: how the partnership's profit is fixed once for all partners and then taxed in each partner's own return. Part 2 is the person who moves to or from Germany during 2026: one assessment as a resident for the whole year, with the foreign income of the other months raising the tax rate. | [de-partners-and-part-year.ANNUAL](#q-cb8c649efbadf0), [de-partners-and-part-year.MOVEINCOME](#q-3c967ac3b81e3f), [de-partners-and-part-year.PARTNER](#q-f58c02a724258f), [de-partners-and-part-year.DETERMINATION](#q-5ff3a234667d07). KG, a GbR or a PartG that runs a business or a profession. Their income is "die Gewinnanteile der Gesellschafter ... und die Vergütungen, die der Gesellschafter von der Gesellschaft für seine Tätigkeit im Dienst der Gesellschaft oder für die Hingabe von Darlehen oder für die Überlassung von Wirtschaftsgütern bezogen hat" (§ 15(1) sentence 1 no. 2 EStG). | 038 | Part 1: partners; LF78–78 | **Step 2: the partner's own return.** The partner takes the determined share into their own return as business income (trade or self-employed), adds any other income, and from there follows de-income-tax: special expenses, tariff, surcharge, church tax. The partner does not recompute the share; if it is wrong, the determination notice is what gets challenged. | [de-partners-and-part-year.BIND](#q-12862cdaedb899), [de-partners-and-part-year.CHALLENGE](#q-4d5a61c03b4fe4), [de-partners-and-part-year.BASE](#q-b72439b1ef0d07). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 039 | Part 1: partners; LF80–80 | **Step 3: trade tax and the § 35 credit.** A commercial partnership pays trade tax itself (§ 5 GewStG; see de-trade-tax). Its trade income starts from the profit fixed in Step 1, Sondervergütungen and special items included (§ 7 GewStG). A partner's share of profit from another partnership is taken out again (§ 9 no. 2 GewStG), because that partnership pays its own trade tax. The base amount, the trade tax actually payable and each partner's share of both are fixed in the same determination (§ 35(2) sentence 1 and (4) EStG). Each partner's share follows the general profit-sharing key, ignoring advance shares (§ 35(2) sentence 2). The BMF letter on § 35 says the same for Sondervergütungen and for results of special and supplementary balance sheets (BMF 3 November 2016, Rn. 22), and a partner that cannot use the credit, such as a GmbH general partner, still takes its key share (Rn. 24). A managing partner's Sondervergütung therefore gives no extra credit. The partner's credit is the lowest of: 4 × share of the base amount; the tax on trade income in the partner's own return (positive trade income / all positive income × tariff tax, § 35(1) sentence 2); the share of trade tax actually payable. Unused credit is lost. | [de-partners-and-part-year.PAYTRADE](#q-cb31acb624b550), [de-partners-and-part-year.TRADEBASE](#q-a679ed4ad9833f), [de-partners-and-part-year.TRADEDIV](#q-a8cfc6338b11c9), [de-partners-and-part-year.PARTCREDIT](#q-80d0f020c0a6ab), [de-partners-and-part-year.MANKEY](#q-82a2a2a4d43187), [de-partners-and-part-year.CREDIT](#q-761804d2b58f3c). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 040 | Part 1: partners; LF82–82 | **Step 4: losses of limited partners (§ 15a EStG).** A limited partner's loss share cannot be set against other income, or carried back or forward under § 10d, so far as it creates or increases a negative capital account. Exception: losses up to the amount by which the registered liability exceeds the contribution actually paid, if the partner is entered in the commercial register, the liability is proven, and a claim under it is not excluded by contract or unlikely (§ 15a(1) sentences 2 and 3). The blocked loss is only set against later profits from the same partnership (§ 15a(2)). It is fixed each year in a separate notice (§ 15a(4)). Withdrawals that create or increase a negative capital account are added as profit only to the extent they are not covered by qualifying outside liability that exists or arises because of those withdrawals under § 15a(1) sentence 2, subject to its register, proof and genuine-exposure conditions. The add-back is capped at the losses deductible in that year and the ten years before (§ 15a(3)). The same rule applies to atypical silent partners and to GbR partners whose liability is excluded or unlikely (§ 15a(5)). | [de-partners-and-part-year.LIMITEDLOSS](#q-7a8d23a80fffbd), [de-partners-and-part-year.LIMITEDSAME](#q-bd8d9c2749116c), [R06.liability](#q-dba8e14c789d2a). Bounded DE-R01–08 wording/evidence successor |
| 041 | Part 1: partners; LF84–84 | **Step 5: the retained-profits option (§ 34a EStG), in summary.** A partner whose profit is computed from balance sheets (§ 4(1) or § 5) and whose share is over 10% or over EUR 10,000 may apply, per partnership share and per year, for profit left in the business to be taxed at 28.25% in 2026 instead of the tariff. Profit left in = profit less the positive balance of withdrawals over contributions, plus trade tax (§ 34a(2)). Exclude withdrawals for the income tax under § 34a(1) sentence 1 and its related solidarity surcharge; withdrawals are deemed used first for these taxes up to their amount (§ 34a(2) sentences 2–3). When later withdrawals exceed later profits, the amount fixed as liable to recapture is taxed at 25% (§ 34a(4)). The surcharge applies to both. The calculation is not set out here: refer. | [de-partners-and-part-year.RETAIN](#q-afc8cd75cf6723), [de-partners-and-part-year.RECAPTURE](#q-a62d29e73a1403), [de-partners-and-part-year.SoliRate](#q-2295800e365bbc), [R06.retained](#q-a12217a3d737d5). Bounded DE-R01–08 wording/evidence successor |

| 050 | Part 2: the year of moving; LF101–101 | **Exception for EU and EEA items under no. 3 only (§ 32b(1) sentence 2).** Treaty-exempt rent from property in another EU or EEA state, profits of an EU or EEA farm, and profits of an EU or EEA commercial establishment that fails the active-business test of § 2a(2), do not raise the rate. The exception is written for no. 3 only. Foreign rent of the months before arrival falls under no. 2 and raises the rate wherever the property is. | [de-partners-and-part-year.PROGEXCEPTIONS](#q-c58197b0e286b4), [de-partners-and-part-year.MOVEINCOME](#q-3c967ac3b81e3f), [de-partners-and-part-year.MANLOSS](#q-25ff210cd382f2). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 051 | Part 2: the year of moving; LF103–103 | **Step 4: the amount added (§ 32b(2)).** Foreign income is computed under German rules: profit or surplus, not gross receipts. For foreign pay, the EUR 1,230 employee lump sum is deducted only to the extent it was not already deducted against German pay, and actual Werbungskosten only so far as all Werbungskosten together exceed the lump sum (§ 32b(2) sentence 2 (a) and (b)). Extraordinary income in it counts at one fifth. Foreign losses lower the rate and can bring it to zero (EStH 2025, H 32b "Ausländische Verluste"). Contributions to the Swiss AHV do not reduce the amount added (same, citing BFH 18 April 2012). | [de-partners-and-part-year.PRORATE](#q-5de21ca647ccc6), [de-partners-and-part-year.MANLOSS](#q-25ff210cd382f2), [de-partners-and-part-year.MANAHV](#q-428e7fe27b5c7d), [de-partners-and-part-year.EMPLOYEE1230](#q-8c6cd06a09983e). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 052 | Part 2: the year of moving; LF105–105 | **Step 5: the tax.** Tariff tax on (taxable income + amount added), divided by that sum, cut to four decimal places of a percent = special rate. Special rate × taxable income, rounded down = income tax. Then the surcharge and church tax on that tax (de-income-tax). | [de-partners-and-part-year.PRORATE](#q-5de21ca647ccc6), [de-partners-and-part-year.MANRATE](#q-27136a828c269f), [de-partners-and-part-year.TARIFF](#q-53ba404bfc89bb). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 053 | Part 2: the year of moving; LF107–107 | **Joint assessment in the year of moving.** Spouses may choose it if both were fully liable and not permanently separated at some time in the year (§ 26(1) EStG). If both move together, the year qualifies. | [de-partners-and-part-year.COUPLE](#q-d2850123d1a652). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |

| 055 | Deadlines and filing; LF113–113 | \| Partnership return \| Erklärung zur gesonderten und einheitlichen Feststellung (main form ESt 1 B, with Anlage FB for the partners and Anlage FE-G or FE-S for the income; names from the ELSTER 2025 guide). A partnership with legal capacity files it, and if it does not, each partner must; for one without legal capacity, each partner (§ 181(2) AO). Electronic only, unless the office allows paper for hardship (§ 181(2a) AO) \| | [de-partners-and-part-year.WHOF](#q-692e23dea14d89), [de-partners-and-part-year.MANFORM](#q-dcd22d50ed5f50). | 062 | Penalties and interest; LF123–123 | - **Late partnership return (§ 152(6) and (7) AO).** The late-filing surcharge rules for tax returns apply. Amount: 0.0625% of the positive sum of the determined income for each started month late, at least EUR 25 a month, rounded down to whole euros, at most EUR 25,000 (§ 152(10) AO). It must be set if the return is not filed within 14 months after the end of the year (for 2026: by 29 February 2028), unless the deadline was extended (§ 152(2) and (3) no. 1 AO, applied by § 152(6)). The nil-tax let-outs of § 152(3) nos. 2 and 3 do not apply to this return. It is set first against the partnership if it has legal capacity (§ 152(4) sentence 3 AO). | [de-partners-and-part-year.PARTLATE](#q-825455b4ad994c), [de-partners-and-part-year.AO152-1](#q-ea9d07a39e5a86), [de-partners-and-part-year.AO152-2](#q-655d3536ec120c), [de-partners-and-part-year.AO152-3](#q-7779b643514559), [de-partners-and-part-year.AO152-10](#q-e0bc17ccffc305). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 063 | Penalties and interest; LF124–124 | - **Partner's own return.** Late filing, interest (0.15% a month from 1 April 2028) and late payment: see de-income-tax. | [de-partners-and-part-year.AO233a-2](#q-ed8a3d4e26461b), [de-partners-and-part-year.AO238-1a](#q-9a3833aea1dadd), [de-partners-and-part-year.AO240-1](#q-1aa98c574a1872). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 064 | Penalties and interest; LF125–125 | - **Interest is charged on the partner, not the partnership.** The determination sets no tax, so § 233a AO interest runs only on each partner's income tax. | [de-partners-and-part-year.AO233a-1](#q-51b5fada9f2177), [de-partners-and-part-year.DETERMINATION](#q-5ff3a234667d07). | 066 | Cross-border points; LF130–130 | - **Foreign partner in a German partnership.** A non-resident partner of a German commercial partnership has German income from a permanent establishment (§ 49(1) no. 2 (a) EStG): de-non-resident-tax. The determination is still made (§ 180(3) no. 1 AO excludes it only where just one partner is liable in Germany). | [de-partners-and-part-year.NRTRADE](#q-f842ffd7862288), [de-partners-and-part-year.ONEPARTNER](#q-12caf76450c1ae). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 067 | Cross-border points; LF131–131 | - **German partner in a foreign partnership.** Treaty-exempt profit is still determined where it matters for the partners' rate (§ 180(5) no. 1 AO) and raises the rate under § 32b(1) no. 3, with the EU/EEA exception for establishments that fail the active-business test. | [de-partners-and-part-year.FOREIGNDET](#q-e055c54c3c05fb), [de-partners-and-part-year.PROGRESSION](#q-ae0d4d64928342), [de-partners-and-part-year.PROGEXCEPTIONS](#q-c58197b0e286b4). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 068 | Cross-border points; LF132–132 | - **Leaving Germany with a partnership share.** A partner's share is business property. Leaving the country can be a deemed withdrawal or a trigger under leaving-germany-exit-tax, which also names a limited partner with more than 25% of the income as having a substantial interest. Refer before the move. | [de-partners-and-part-year.PARTNER](#q-f58c02a724258f). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 069 | Cross-border points; LF133–133 | - **Arrival with foreign pay or rent.** Use Part 2. Foreign tax paid on income of the other period gives no credit in Germany, because Germany does not tax that income; it only raises the rate. | [de-partners-and-part-year.MOVEINCOME](#q-3c967ac3b81e3f), [de-partners-and-part-year.FOREIGNCREDIT](#q-00fba155716cab). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 070 | Cross-border points; LF134–134 | - **Social security.** Contributions linked to pay that a treaty exempts in Germany (the § 32b(1) no. 3 case) are not special expenses (§ 10(2) sentence 1 no. 1 EStG; BMF letter on Vorsorgeaufwendungen, Rn. 199), unless the pay was earned in an EU or EEA state or Switzerland and that state allows no deduction for them (§ 10(2) sentence 1 no. 1 (a) to (c)). Contributions on foreign pay of the months before arrival or after departure (the no. 2 case) are not settled here (see Not yet confirmed). Swiss AHV contributions do not reduce the amount added for the rate (H 32b). Social security itself: de-social-contributions. | [de-partners-and-part-year.INSFOREIGN](#q-24bd5db2ed9aa1), [de-partners-and-part-year.MANINS](#q-552aacddad9c7b), [de-partners-and-part-year.MANAHV](#q-428e7fe27b5c7d). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |

| 178 | DE-R01–08 added ordinary method / intake / selected source; LF38–38 | **Separable own commercial side activity: annual net-receipt test.** Under the BFH interpretation adopted in BMF EStH 2025 H 15.8(5), an otherwise noncommercial partnership's separable own commercial activity does not reclassify the rest if BOTH `commercial net receipts ≤ 0.03 × total partnership net receipts` AND `commercial net receipts ≤ EUR 24,500` for the assessment period. Total receipts include the commercial and noncommercial activities, excluding VAT. Apply the same test whether the commercial branch makes a profit or a loss. The branch remains commercial in its own right; an inseparably commercial overall activity does not become a free profession under this exception. This own-activity test does not apply to commercial income received through a participation in another partnership; keep that income-tax limb and its separate trade-tax treatment distinct. | [R05.statute](#q-700ac627324e9e), [R05.BFH-separability](#q-f8f08ca142481e), [R05.BFH-thresholds](#q-4021323d855189), [R05.BMF-current](#q-288eaa88f2fa05), [R05.BFH-2022](#q-3c71f946f6d567). New bounded review-finding correction |
| 179 | DE-R01–08 added ordinary method / intake / selected source; LF40–40 | For an otherwise qualifying separable professional partnership, total net receipts of EUR 500,000 and commercial net receipts of EUR 15,000 pass both gates exactly; EUR 15,000.01 fails the 3% gate. At total net receipts of EUR 1,000,000, commercial receipts of EUR 24,500 pass both; EUR 24,500.01 fails the absolute gate although below 3%. These are receipts tests, not a profit allowance. The outcome under these two gates is unchanged if the commercial branch has a loss. | [R05.statute](#q-700ac627324e9e), [R05.BFH-separability](#q-f8f08ca142481e), [R05.BFH-thresholds](#q-4021323d855189), [R05.BMF-current](#q-288eaa88f2fa05), [R05.BFH-2022](#q-3c71f946f6d567). ## Complete old-topic destinations

The exact original old-source units, hashes/wording and all predecessor destinations are preserved; successor coordinates are derived mechanically from the pinned native line map. Corrected universal statements are historical claims, not reinstated law.

| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF15 | This skill covers two gaps the other Germany skills leave open. Part 1 is the partner in a partnership: how the partnership's profit is fixed once for all partners and then taxed in each partner's own return. Part 2 is the person who moves to or from Germany during 2026: one assessment as a resident for the whole year, with the foreign income of the other months raising the tax rate. | Exact wording retained at current LF18, Germany: partners' profit shares and the year of moving to or from Germany, tax year 2026  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF17 | See also, and do not repeat here: | Exact wording retained at current LF20, Germany: partners' profit shares and the year of moving to or from Germany, tax year 2026  |

| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF29 | - **In scope.** A person resident in Germany (§ 1(1) EStG) who is a co-entrepreneur (Mitunternehmer) of a partnership: a partner in an OHG, a KG (general or limited partner), a GmbH & Co. KG, a GbR or a PartG that runs a business or a profession. Their income is "die Gewinnanteile der Gesellschafter ... und die Vergütungen, die der Gesellschafter von der Gesellschaft für seine Tätigkeit im Dienst der Gesellschaft oder für die Hingabe von Darlehen oder für die Überlassung von Wirtschaftsgütern bezogen hat" (§ 15(1) sentence 1 no. 2 EStG). A partner holding through another partnership is treated the same way (§ 15(1) sentence 1 no. 2 sentence 2). | Exact wording retained at current LF32, Who this applies to  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF30 | - **Trade or profession.** A partnership whose partners all practise a free profession (§ 18(1) no. 1 EStG: doctors, lawyers, engineers, architects, tax advisers and the like) has income from self-employed work; § 18(4) applies § 15(1) no. 2 and § 15a to it, so the same profit share and Sondervergütung rules apply. It pays no trade tax unless one of two rules makes the whole activity a trade: | Exact wording retained at current LF33, Who this applies to  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF31 |   - **Abfärbung (§ 15(3) no. 1 EStG).** Any commercial activity of its own, or commercial income from a share in another partnership, makes the whole activity commercial, whether that part makes a profit or a loss. | Exact wording retained at current LF34, Who this applies to DE-R05: universal own-activity contamination corrected to separable dual net-receipt gates; participation limb separate. |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF32 |   - **Gewerbliche Prägung (§ 15(3) no. 2 EStG).** A partnership with no commercial activity whose only personally liable partners are capital companies, managed only by them or by non-partners. A GmbH & Co. KG run by its GmbH is the usual case. | Exact wording retained at current LF35, Who this applies to  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF33 | - **Out of scope.** Partnerships that opted to be taxed as a company (§ 1a KStG); partners that are companies (de-corporate-tax); sale or closing of a partnership share (§§ 16, 34 EStG); a change of partners during the year; foreign partnerships. Refer. | Exact wording retained at current LF36, Who this applies to  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF35 | **Part 2, the year of moving.** | Exact wording retained at current LF42, Who this applies to  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF37 | - **In scope.** An individual who, in 2026, takes up or gives up a German home or habitual abode, so that they are fully liable for part of the year and either limited liable or not liable at all for the rest. Arrivals and departures alike. | Exact wording retained at current LF44, Who this applies to  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF38 | - **The rule.** Income tax is an annual tax, assessed per calendar year (§ 2(7) sentences 1 and 2 EStG). "Besteht während eines Kalenderjahres sowohl unbeschränkte als auch beschränkte Einkommensteuerpflicht, so sind die während der beschränkten Einkommensteuerpflicht erzielten inländischen Einkünfte in eine Veranlagung zur unbeschränkten Einkommensteuerpflicht einzubeziehen" (§ 2(7) sentence 3). So there is one assessment as a resident, covering the whole year. | Exact wording retained at current LF45, Who this applies to  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF39 | - **Out of scope.** The exit tax on shares when leaving (leaving-germany-exit-tax); a person who never becomes resident (de-non-resident-tax); the extended limited liability under § 2 AStG. | Exact wording retained at current LF46, Who this applies to  |

| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF63 | **Step 1: the partnership's profit is fixed once.** Where several people share in income, it is fixed separately and uniformly for all of them (§ 180(1) sentence 1 no. 2 (a) AO; § 179(2) AO). The determination notice (Feststellungsbescheid) binds each partner's own assessment. It fixes: | Exact wording retained at current LF70, Part 1: partners  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF65 | 1. **The partnership's own profit** (Gesamthandsgewinn), from its books or cash accounts. Trade tax is not deductible (§ 4(5b) EStG). | Exact wording retained at current LF72, Part 1: partners  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF66 | 2. **Each partner's Sondervergütungen**: pay for work for the partnership, interest on loans to it, rent for assets let to it (§ 15(1) no. 2 EStG). If the partnership booked them as costs, they are added back to that partner. They are business income, not wages, interest or rent. | Exact wording retained at current LF73, Part 1: partners  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF67 | 3. **Each partner's special business income and costs** (Sonderbetriebseinnahmen and -ausgaben): for example interest on a loan the partner took to buy the share, or the partner's own costs on a building let to the partnership. Each partner reports these to the partnership so they go into the determination. | Exact wording retained at current LF74, Part 1: partners  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF68 | 4. **The split.** Profit share by the partnership agreement, plus the partner's own items from 2 and 3. | Exact wording retained at current LF75, Part 1: partners  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF69 | 5. **Other items**: income exempt under a treaty that matters for the partners' rate (§ 180(5) no. 1 AO), and tax withheld to be credited (§ 180(5) no. 2 AO); for a commercial partnership, the § 35 figures (Step 3). | Exact wording retained at current LF76, Part 1: partners  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF71 | **Step 2: the partner's own return.** The partner takes the determined share into their own return as business income (trade or self-employed), adds any other income, and from there follows de-income-tax: special expenses, tariff, surcharge, church tax. The partner does not recompute the share; if it is wrong, the determination notice is what gets challenged. | Exact wording retained at current LF78, Part 1: partners  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF73 | **Step 3: trade tax and the § 35 credit.** A commercial partnership pays trade tax itself (§ 5 GewStG; see de-trade-tax). Its trade income starts from the profit fixed in Step 1, Sondervergütungen and special items included (§ 7 GewStG). A partner's share of profit from another partnership is taken out again (§ 9 no. 2 GewStG), because that partnership pays its own trade tax. The base amount, the trade tax actually payable and each partner's share of both are fixed in the same determination (§ 35(2) sentence 1 and (4) EStG). Each partner's share follows the general profit-sharing key, ignoring advance shares (§ 35(2) sentence 2). The BMF letter on § 35 says the same for Sondervergütungen and for results of special and supplementary balance sheets (BMF 3 November 2016, Rn. 22), and a partner that cannot use the credit, such as a GmbH general partner, still takes its key share (Rn. 24). A managing partner's Sondervergütung therefore gives no extra credit. The partner's credit is the lowest of: 4 × share of the base amount; the tax on trade income in the partner's own return (positive trade income / all positive income × tariff tax, § 35(1) sentence 2); the share of trade tax actually payable. Unused credit is lost. | Exact wording retained at current LF80, Part 1: partners  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF75 | **Step 4: losses of limited partners (§ 15a EStG).** A limited partner's loss share cannot be set against other income, or carried back or forward under § 10d, so far as it creates or increases a negative capital account. Exception: losses up to the amount by which the registered liability exceeds the contribution actually paid, if the partner is entered in the commercial register, the liability is proven, and a claim under it is not excluded by contract or unlikely (§ 15a(1) sentences 2 and 3). The blocked loss is only set against later profits from the same partnership (§ 15a(2)). It is fixed each year in a separate notice (§ 15a(4)). Withdrawals that create or increase a negative capital account are added as profit, up to the losses that were deductible in that year and the ten years before (§ 15a(3)). The same rule applies to atypical silent partners and to GbR partners whose liability is excluded or unlikely (§ 15a(5)). | Exact wording retained at current LF82, Part 1: partners DE-R06: statutory tax/Soli withdrawal priority/exclusion and qualifying outside-liability exception preserved in summary; detailed-regime referral retained. |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF77 | **Step 5: the retained-profits option (§ 34a EStG), in summary.** A partner whose profit is computed from balance sheets (§ 4(1) or § 5) and whose share is over 10% or over EUR 10,000 may apply, per partnership share and per year, for profit left in the business to be taxed at 28.25% in 2026 instead of the tariff. Profit left in = profit less the positive balance of withdrawals over contributions, plus trade tax (§ 34a(2)). When later withdrawals exceed later profits, the amount fixed as liable to recapture is taxed at 25% (§ 34a(4)). The surcharge applies to both. The calculation is not set out here: refer. | Exact wording retained at current LF84, Part 1: partners DE-R06: statutory tax/Soli withdrawal priority/exclusion and qualifying outside-liability exception preserved in summary; detailed-regime referral retained. |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF81 | **Step 1: split the year.** Fix the date the German home or habitual abode began or ended. The months before arrival or after departure are the "other period". | Exact wording retained at current LF88, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF83 | **Step 2: the taxable base.** It contains: | Exact wording retained at current LF90, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF85 | - all income of the resident months, worldwide (§ 2(1) EStG: income earned "während seiner unbeschränkten Einkommensteuerpflicht"); | Exact wording retained at current LF92, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF86 | - German-source income of the other period (§ 49 EStG; see de-non-resident-tax), for example rent from a German flat let before arrival (§ 2(7) sentence 3); | Exact wording retained at current LF93, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF87 | - the full basic allowance and the full employee lump sum: there is no cut by months for a resident assessment. Special expenses of the resident months are deducted as usual; for contributions paid in the other months see Cross-border points. | Exact wording retained at current LF94, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF89 | **Step 3: income that raises the rate.** These are left out of the base but added to find the rate (§ 32b(1) sentence 1): | Exact wording retained at current LF96, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF91 | - **No. 2: foreign income of the other period** that was not subject to German tax ("ausländische Einkünfte, die im Veranlagungszeitraum nicht der deutschen Einkommensteuer unterlegen haben"), for cases of part-year full liability, including § 2(7) sentence 3 cases. The official guidance confirms: on arrival or departure, income earned outside the period of full liability counts for the progression clause if it is not subject to limited liability (EStH 2025, H 32b "Zeitweise unbeschränkte Steuerpflicht"). | Exact wording retained at current LF98, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF92 | - **No. 3: income a tax treaty exempts in Germany**, in the resident months (often foreign employment income or foreign rent). | Exact wording retained at current LF99, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF94 | **Exception for EU and EEA items under no. 3 only (§ 32b(1) sentence 2).** Treaty-exempt rent from property in another EU or EEA state, profits of an EU or EEA farm, and profits of an EU or EEA commercial establishment that fails the active-business test of § 2a(2), do not raise the rate. The exception is written for no. 3 only. Foreign rent of the months before arrival falls under no. 2 and raises the rate wherever the property is. | Exact wording retained at current LF101, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF96 | **Step 4: the amount added (§ 32b(2)).** Foreign income is computed under German rules: profit or surplus, not gross receipts. For foreign pay, the EUR 1,230 employee lump sum is deducted only to the extent it was not already deducted against German pay, and actual Werbungskosten only so far as all Werbungskosten together exceed the lump sum (§ 32b(2) sentence 2 (a) and (b)). Extraordinary income in it counts at one fifth. Foreign losses lower the rate and can bring it to zero (EStH 2025, H 32b "Ausländische Verluste"). Contributions to the Swiss AHV do not reduce the amount added (same, citing BFH 18 April 2012). | Exact wording retained at current LF103, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF98 | **Step 5: the tax.** Tariff tax on (taxable income + amount added), divided by that sum, cut to four decimal places of a percent = special rate. Special rate × taxable income, rounded down = income tax. Then the surcharge and church tax on that tax (de-income-tax). | Exact wording retained at current LF105, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF100 | **Joint assessment in the year of moving.** Spouses may choose it if both were fully liable and not permanently separated at some time in the year (§ 26(1) EStG). If both move together, the year qualifies. | Exact wording retained at current LF107, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF104 | \| Step \| Rule \| | Exact wording retained at current LF111, Deadlines and filing  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF106 | \| Partnership return \| Erklärung zur gesonderten und einheitlichen Feststellung (main form ESt 1 B, with Anlage FB for the partners and Anlage FE-G or FE-S for the income; names from the ELSTER 2025 guide). A partnership with legal capacity files it, and if it does not, each partner must; for one without legal capacity, each partner (§ 181(2) AO). Electronic only, unless the office allows paper for hardship (§ 181(2a) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF113, Deadlines and filing  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF107 | \| Partnership return deadline, own preparation \| Seven months after year end (§ 149(2) AO, applied by § 181(1) AO): 31 July 2027 is a Saturday, so Monday 2 August 2027 (§ 108(3) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF114, Deadlines and filing  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF108 | \| Partnership return deadline, tax adviser \| Last day of February of the second year after: 29 February 2028 (§ 149(3) no. 5 AO). The office can ask for it earlier (§ 149(4) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF115, Deadlines and filing  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF109 | \| Trade tax return of a commercial partnership \| Same deadlines (§ 149(3) no. 3 AO); see de-trade-tax \| | Exact wording retained at current LF116, Deadlines and filing  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF110 | \| Partner's own return \| Deadlines in de-income-tax (2 August 2027, or 29 February 2028 with an adviser) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF117, Deadlines and filing  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF111 | \| Changes flow through \| A change to the determination changes the partner's assessment; a change to profit changes the trade tax base amount notice (§ 35b(1) GewStG) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF118, Deadlines and filing  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF112 | \| Year of moving \| One return for the whole year, on the normal deadlines in de-income-tax. Foreign pay of the other period goes on Anlage N-AUS (de-employee-income-tax). Arrival and departure dates and foreign income must be declared \| | Exact wording retained at current LF119, Deadlines and filing  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF116 | - **Late partnership return (§ 152(6) and (7) AO).** The late-filing surcharge rules for tax returns apply. Amount: 0.0625% of the positive sum of the determined income for each started month late, at least EUR 25 a month, rounded down to whole euros, at most EUR 25,000 (§ 152(10) AO). It must be set if the return is not filed within 14 months after the end of the year (for 2026: by 29 February 2028), unless the deadline was extended (§ 152(2) and (3) no. 1 AO, applied by § 152(6)). The nil-tax let-outs of § 152(3) nos. 2 and 3 do not apply to this return. It is set first against the partnership if it has legal capacity (§ 152(4) sentence 3 AO). | Exact wording retained at current LF123, Penalties and interest  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF117 | - **Partner's own return.** Late filing, interest (0.15% a month from 1 April 2028) and late payment: see de-income-tax. | Exact wording retained at current LF124, Penalties and interest  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF118 | - **Interest is charged on the partner, not the partnership.** The determination sets no tax, so § 233a AO interest runs only on each partner's income tax. | Exact wording retained at current LF125, Penalties and interest  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF119 | - **Wrong figures.** Tax evasion (§ 370 AO) and reckless understatement (§ 378 AO): refer. | Exact wording retained at current LF126, Penalties and interest  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF123 | - **Foreign partner in a German partnership.** A non-resident partner of a German commercial partnership has German income from a permanent establishment (§ 49(1) no. 2 (a) EStG): de-non-resident-tax. The determination is still made (§ 180(3) no. 1 AO excludes it only where just one partner is liable in Germany). | Exact wording retained at current LF130, Cross-border points  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF124 | - **German partner in a foreign partnership.** Treaty-exempt profit is still determined where it matters for the partners' rate (§ 180(5) no. 1 AO) and raises the rate under § 32b(1) no. 3, with the EU/EEA exception for establishments that fail the active-business test. | Exact wording retained at current LF131, Cross-border points  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF125 | - **Leaving Germany with a partnership share.** A partner's share is business property. Leaving the country can be a deemed withdrawal or a trigger under leaving-germany-exit-tax, which also names a limited partner with more than 25% of the income as having a substantial interest. Refer before the move. | Exact wording retained at current LF132, Cross-border points DE-R06: statutory tax/Soli withdrawal priority/exclusion and qualifying outside-liability exception preserved in summary; detailed-regime referral retained. |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF126 | - **Arrival with foreign pay or rent.** Use Part 2. Foreign tax paid on income of the other period gives no credit in Germany, because Germany does not tax that income; it only raises the rate. | Exact wording retained at current LF133, Cross-border points  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF127 | - **Social security.** Contributions linked to pay that a treaty exempts in Germany (the § 32b(1) no. 3 case) are not special expenses (§ 10(2) sentence 1 no. 1 EStG; BMF letter on Vorsorgeaufwendungen, Rn. 199), unless the pay was earned in an EU or EEA state or Switzerland and that state allows no deduction for them (§ 10(2) sentence 1 no. 1 (a) to (c)). Contributions on foreign pay of the months before arrival or after departure (the no. 2 case) are not settled here (see Not yet confirmed). Swiss AHV contributions do not reduce the amount added for the rate (H 32b). Social security itself: de-social-contributions. | Exact wording retained at current LF134, Cross-border points  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF133 | The Alpha GmbH & Co. KG runs a wholesale business in a town with a trade tax multiplier of 380%. Its general partner is a GmbH with no share in profit. Two limited partners: Anna (60%) and Ben (40%). In 2026 the KG's own profit, after booking Anna's management pay as a cost and before trade tax, is EUR 300,000. Anna's management pay from the KG: EUR 120,000 (a Sondervergütung). Anna pays EUR 4,000 interest on a loan she took to buy her share (a Sonderbetriebsausgabe). No trade tax add-backs or reductions. Anna is single, no church, no other income, and her deductible insurance is EUR 15,000. (Invented case; the multiplier is an input.) | Exact wording retained at current LF140, Example 1: managing limited partner of a GmbH & Co. KG  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF135 | - KG's trade income: 300,000 + 120,000 − 4,000 = EUR 416,000. Less EUR 24,500 = 391,500 × 3.5% = base amount EUR 13,702.50. Trade tax: × 380% = EUR 52,069.50. | Exact wording retained at current LF142, Example 1: managing limited partner of a GmbH & Co. KG  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF136 | - Anna's share: 60% × 300,000 + 120,000 − 4,000 = EUR 296,000. Ben's: EUR 120,000. | Exact wording retained at current LF143, Example 1: managing limited partner of a GmbH & Co. KG  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF137 | - Anna's share of the base amount by the general key (60.00%), the pay ignored: EUR 8,221.50. Of trade tax payable: EUR 31,241.70. | Exact wording retained at current LF144, Example 1: managing limited partner of a GmbH & Co. KG  |
| b0e37140e1383d6e8628f6d4d8e8b63831b8703a LF138 | - Anna's taxable income: 296,000 − 15,000 − 36 = EUR 280,964. Zone 5: 0.45 × 280,964 − 19,470.38 = 106,963.42, so EUR 106,963. | Exact wording retained at current LF145, Example 1: managing limited partner of a GmbH & Co. KG  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF18 | This skill covers two gaps the other Germany skills leave open. Part 1 is the partner in a partnership: how the partnership's profit is fixed once for all partners and then taxed in each partner's own return. Part 2 is the person who moves to or from Germany during 2026: one assessment as a resident for the whole year, with the foreign income of the other months raising the tax rate. | Exact wording retained at current LF18, Germany: partners' profit shares and the year of moving to or from Germany, tax year 2026  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF20 | See also, and do not repeat here: | Exact wording retained at current LF20, Germany: partners' profit shares and the year of moving to or from Germany, tax year 2026  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF22 | - **de-income-tax**: the 2026 tariff, special expenses, solidarity surcharge, church tax, the § 35 credit for a sole trader, deadlines and penalties for the partner's own return. | Exact wording retained at current LF22, Germany: partners' profit shares and the year of moving to or from Germany, tax year 2026  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF23 | - **de-trade-tax**: trade tax at partnership level (add-backs, reductions, the EUR 24,500 allowance, multiplier), Abfärbung and gewerbliche Prägung in detail. | Exact wording retained at current LF23, Germany: partners' profit shares and the year of moving to or from Germany, tax year 2026 DE-R05: universal own-activity contamination corrected to separable dual net-receipt gates; participation limb separate. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF24 | - **de-employee-income-tax**: wages, Werbungskosten, joint assessment, Anlage N-AUS. | Exact wording retained at current LF24, Germany: partners' profit shares and the year of moving to or from Germany, tax year 2026  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF25 | - **de-non-resident-tax**: limited liability, German-source income (§ 49 EStG), the § 1(3) option, foreign tax credits (§ 34c EStG). | Exact wording retained at current LF25, Germany: partners' profit shares and the year of moving to or from Germany, tax year 2026  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF26 | - **leaving-germany-exit-tax**: § 6 AStG on shares and the extended limited liability after a move. | Exact wording retained at current LF26, Germany: partners' profit shares and the year of moving to or from Germany, tax year 2026  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF30 | **Part 1, partners.** | Exact wording retained at current LF30, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF32 | - **In scope.** A person resident in Germany (§ 1(1) EStG) who is a co-entrepreneur (Mitunternehmer) of a partnership: a partner in an OHG, a KG (general or limited partner), a GmbH & Co. KG, a GbR or a PartG that runs a business or a profession. Their income is "die Gewinnanteile der Gesellschafter ... und die Vergütungen, die der Gesellschafter von der Gesellschaft für seine Tätigkeit im Dienst der Gesellschaft oder für die Hingabe von Darlehen oder für die Überlassung von Wirtschaftsgütern bezogen hat" (§ 15(1) sentence 1 no. 2 EStG). A partner holding through another partnership is treated the same way (§ 15(1) sentence 1 no. 2 sentence 2). | Exact wording retained at current LF32, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF33 | - **Trade or profession.** A partnership whose partners all practise a free profession (§ 18(1) no. 1 EStG: doctors, lawyers, engineers, architects, tax advisers and the like) has income from self-employed work; § 18(4) applies § 15(1) no. 2 and § 15a to it, so the same profit share and Sondervergütung rules apply. It pays no trade tax unless one of two rules makes the whole activity a trade: | Exact wording retained at current LF33, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF34 |   - **Abfärbung (§ 15(3) no. 1 EStG).** Any commercial activity of its own, or commercial income from a share in another partnership, makes the whole activity commercial, whether that part makes a profit or a loss. | Exact wording retained at current LF34, Who this applies to DE-R05: universal own-activity contamination corrected to separable dual net-receipt gates; participation limb separate. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF35 |   - **Gewerbliche Prägung (§ 15(3) no. 2 EStG).** A partnership with no commercial activity whose only personally liable partners are capital companies, managed only by them or by non-partners. A GmbH & Co. KG run by its GmbH is the usual case. | Exact wording retained at current LF35, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF36 | - **Out of scope.** Partnerships that opted to be taxed as a company (§ 1a KStG); partners that are companies (de-corporate-tax); sale or closing of a partnership share (§§ 16, 34 EStG); a change of partners during the year; foreign partnerships. Refer. | Exact wording retained at current LF36, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF38 | **Part 2, the year of moving.** | Exact wording retained at current LF42, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF40 | - **In scope.** An individual who, in 2026, takes up or gives up a German home or habitual abode, so that they are fully liable for part of the year and either limited liable or not liable at all for the rest. Arrivals and departures alike. | Exact wording retained at current LF44, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF41 | - **The rule.** Income tax is an annual tax, assessed per calendar year (§ 2(7) sentences 1 and 2 EStG). "Besteht während eines Kalenderjahres sowohl unbeschränkte als auch beschränkte Einkommensteuerpflicht, so sind die während der beschränkten Einkommensteuerpflicht erzielten inländischen Einkünfte in eine Veranlagung zur unbeschränkten Einkommensteuerpflicht einzubeziehen" (§ 2(7) sentence 3). So there is one assessment as a resident, covering the whole year. | Exact wording retained at current LF45, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF42 | - **Out of scope.** The exit tax on shares when leaving (leaving-germany-exit-tax); a person who never becomes resident (de-non-resident-tax); the extended limited liability under § 2 AStG. | Exact wording retained at current LF46, Who this applies to  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF66 | **Step 1: the partnership's profit is fixed once.** Where several people share in income, it is fixed separately and uniformly for all of them (§ 180(1) sentence 1 no. 2 (a) AO; § 179(2) AO). The determination notice (Feststellungsbescheid) binds each partner's own assessment. It fixes: | Exact wording retained at current LF70, Part 1: partners  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF68 | 1. **The partnership's own profit** (Gesamthandsgewinn), from its books or cash accounts. Trade tax is not deductible (§ 4(5b) EStG). | Exact wording retained at current LF72, Part 1: partners  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF69 | 2. **Each partner's Sondervergütungen**: pay for work for the partnership, interest on loans to it, rent for assets let to it (§ 15(1) no. 2 EStG). If the partnership booked them as costs, they are added back to that partner. They are business income, not wages, interest or rent. | Exact wording retained at current LF73, Part 1: partners  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF70 | 3. **Each partner's special business income and costs** (Sonderbetriebseinnahmen and -ausgaben): for example interest on a loan the partner took to buy the share, or the partner's own costs on a building let to the partnership. Each partner reports these to the partnership so they go into the determination. | Exact wording retained at current LF74, Part 1: partners  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF71 | 4. **The split.** Profit share by the partnership agreement, plus the partner's own items from 2 and 3. | Exact wording retained at current LF75, Part 1: partners  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF72 | 5. **Other items**: income exempt under a treaty that matters for the partners' rate (§ 180(5) no. 1 AO), and tax withheld to be credited (§ 180(5) no. 2 AO); for a commercial partnership, the § 35 figures (Step 3). | Exact wording retained at current LF76, Part 1: partners  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF74 | **Step 2: the partner's own return.** The partner takes the determined share into their own return as business income (trade or self-employed), adds any other income, and from there follows de-income-tax: special expenses, tariff, surcharge, church tax. The partner does not recompute the share; if it is wrong, the determination notice is what gets challenged. | Exact wording retained at current LF78, Part 1: partners  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF76 | **Step 3: trade tax and the § 35 credit.** A commercial partnership pays trade tax itself (§ 5 GewStG; see de-trade-tax). Its trade income starts from the profit fixed in Step 1, Sondervergütungen and special items included (§ 7 GewStG). A partner's share of profit from another partnership is taken out again (§ 9 no. 2 GewStG), because that partnership pays its own trade tax. The base amount, the trade tax actually payable and each partner's share of both are fixed in the same determination (§ 35(2) sentence 1 and (4) EStG). Each partner's share follows the general profit-sharing key, ignoring advance shares (§ 35(2) sentence 2). The BMF letter on § 35 says the same for Sondervergütungen and for results of special and supplementary balance sheets (BMF 3 November 2016, Rn. 22), and a partner that cannot use the credit, such as a GmbH general partner, still takes its key share (Rn. 24). A managing partner's Sondervergütung therefore gives no extra credit. The partner's credit is the lowest of: 4 × share of the base amount; the tax on trade income in the partner's own return (positive trade income / all positive income × tariff tax, § 35(1) sentence 2); the share of trade tax actually payable. Unused credit is lost. | Exact wording retained at current LF80, Part 1: partners  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF78 | **Step 4: losses of limited partners (§ 15a EStG).** A limited partner's loss share cannot be set against other income, or carried back or forward under § 10d, so far as it creates or increases a negative capital account. Exception: losses up to the amount by which the registered liability exceeds the contribution actually paid, if the partner is entered in the commercial register, the liability is proven, and a claim under it is not excluded by contract or unlikely (§ 15a(1) sentences 2 and 3). The blocked loss is only set against later profits from the same partnership (§ 15a(2)). It is fixed each year in a separate notice (§ 15a(4)). Withdrawals that create or increase a negative capital account are added as profit, up to the losses that were deductible in that year and the ten years before (§ 15a(3)). The same rule applies to atypical silent partners and to GbR partners whose liability is excluded or unlikely (§ 15a(5)). | Exact wording retained at current LF82, Part 1: partners DE-R06: statutory tax/Soli withdrawal priority/exclusion and qualifying outside-liability exception preserved in summary; detailed-regime referral retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF80 | **Step 5: the retained-profits option (§ 34a EStG), in summary.** A partner whose profit is computed from balance sheets (§ 4(1) or § 5) and whose share is over 10% or over EUR 10,000 may apply, per partnership share and per year, for profit left in the business to be taxed at 28.25% in 2026 instead of the tariff. Profit left in = profit less the positive balance of withdrawals over contributions, plus trade tax (§ 34a(2)). When later withdrawals exceed later profits, the amount fixed as liable to recapture is taxed at 25% (§ 34a(4)). The surcharge applies to both. The calculation is not set out here: refer. | Exact wording retained at current LF84, Part 1: partners DE-R06: statutory tax/Soli withdrawal priority/exclusion and qualifying outside-liability exception preserved in summary; detailed-regime referral retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF84 | **Step 1: split the year.** Fix the date the German home or habitual abode began or ended. The months before arrival or after departure are the "other period". | Exact wording retained at current LF88, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF86 | **Step 2: the taxable base.** It contains: | Exact wording retained at current LF90, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF88 | - all income of the resident months, worldwide (§ 2(1) EStG: income earned "während seiner unbeschränkten Einkommensteuerpflicht"); | Exact wording retained at current LF92, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF89 | - German-source income of the other period (§ 49 EStG; see de-non-resident-tax), for example rent from a German flat let before arrival (§ 2(7) sentence 3); | Exact wording retained at current LF93, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF90 | - the full basic allowance and the full employee lump sum: there is no cut by months for a resident assessment. Special expenses of the resident months are deducted as usual; for contributions paid in the other months see Cross-border points. | Exact wording retained at current LF94, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF92 | **Step 3: income that raises the rate.** These are left out of the base but added to find the rate (§ 32b(1) sentence 1): | Exact wording retained at current LF96, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF94 | - **No. 2: foreign income of the other period** that was not subject to German tax ("ausländische Einkünfte, die im Veranlagungszeitraum nicht der deutschen Einkommensteuer unterlegen haben"), for cases of part-year full liability, including § 2(7) sentence 3 cases. The official guidance confirms: on arrival or departure, income earned outside the period of full liability counts for the progression clause if it is not subject to limited liability (EStH 2025, H 32b "Zeitweise unbeschränkte Steuerpflicht"). | Exact wording retained at current LF98, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF95 | - **No. 3: income a tax treaty exempts in Germany**, in the resident months (often foreign employment income or foreign rent). | Exact wording retained at current LF99, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF97 | **Exception for EU and EEA items under no. 3 only (§ 32b(1) sentence 2).** Treaty-exempt rent from property in another EU or EEA state, profits of an EU or EEA farm, and profits of an EU or EEA commercial establishment that fails the active-business test of § 2a(2), do not raise the rate. The exception is written for no. 3 only. Foreign rent of the months before arrival falls under no. 2 and raises the rate wherever the property is. | Exact wording retained at current LF101, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF99 | **Step 4: the amount added (§ 32b(2)).** Foreign income is computed under German rules: profit or surplus, not gross receipts. For foreign pay, the EUR 1,230 employee lump sum is deducted only to the extent it was not already deducted against German pay, and actual Werbungskosten only so far as all Werbungskosten together exceed the lump sum (§ 32b(2) sentence 2 (a) and (b)). Extraordinary income in it counts at one fifth. Foreign losses lower the rate and can bring it to zero (EStH 2025, H 32b "Ausländische Verluste"). Contributions to the Swiss AHV do not reduce the amount added (same, citing BFH 18 April 2012). | Exact wording retained at current LF103, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF101 | **Step 5: the tax.** Tariff tax on (taxable income + amount added), divided by that sum, cut to four decimal places of a percent = special rate. Special rate × taxable income, rounded down = income tax. Then the surcharge and church tax on that tax (de-income-tax). | Exact wording retained at current LF105, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF103 | **Joint assessment in the year of moving.** Spouses may choose it if both were fully liable and not permanently separated at some time in the year (§ 26(1) EStG). If both move together, the year qualifies. | Exact wording retained at current LF107, Part 2: the year of moving  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF107 | \| Step \| Rule \| | Exact wording retained at current LF111, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF109 | \| Partnership return \| Erklärung zur gesonderten und einheitlichen Feststellung (main form ESt 1 B, with Anlage FB for the partners and Anlage FE-G or FE-S for the income; names from the ELSTER 2025 guide). A partnership with legal capacity files it, and if it does not, each partner must; for one without legal capacity, each partner (§ 181(2) AO). Electronic only, unless the office allows paper for hardship (§ 181(2a) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF113, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF110 | \| Partnership return deadline, own preparation \| Seven months after year end (§ 149(2) AO, applied by § 181(1) AO): 31 July 2027 is a Saturday, so Monday 2 August 2027 (§ 108(3) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF114, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF111 | \| Partnership return deadline, tax adviser \| Last day of February of the second year after: 29 February 2028 (§ 149(3) no. 5 AO). The office can ask for it earlier (§ 149(4) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF115, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF112 | \| Trade tax return of a commercial partnership \| Same deadlines (§ 149(3) no. 3 AO); see de-trade-tax \| | Exact wording retained at current LF116, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF113 | \| Partner's own return \| Deadlines in de-income-tax (2 August 2027, or 29 February 2028 with an adviser) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF117, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF114 | \| Changes flow through \| A change to the determination changes the partner's assessment; a change to profit changes the trade tax base amount notice (§ 35b(1) GewStG) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF118, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF115 | \| Year of moving \| One return for the whole year, on the normal deadlines in de-income-tax. Foreign pay of the other period goes on Anlage N-AUS (de-employee-income-tax). Arrival and departure dates and foreign income must be declared \| | Exact wording retained at current LF119, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF119 | - **Late partnership return (§ 152(6) and (7) AO).** The late-filing surcharge rules for tax returns apply. Amount: 0.0625% of the positive sum of the determined income for each started month late, at least EUR 25 a month, rounded down to whole euros, at most EUR 25,000 (§ 152(10) AO). It must be set if the return is not filed within 14 months after the end of the year (for 2026: by 29 February 2028), unless the deadline was extended (§ 152(2) and (3) no. 1 AO, applied by § 152(6)). The nil-tax let-outs of § 152(3) nos. 2 and 3 do not apply to this return. It is set first against the partnership if it has legal capacity (§ 152(4) sentence 3 AO). | Exact wording retained at current LF123, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF120 | - **Partner's own return.** Late filing, interest (0.15% a month from 1 April 2028) and late payment: see de-income-tax. | Exact wording retained at current LF124, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF121 | - **Interest is charged on the partner, not the partnership.** The determination sets no tax, so § 233a AO interest runs only on each partner's income tax. | Exact wording retained at current LF125, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF122 | - **Wrong figures.** Tax evasion (§ 370 AO) and reckless understatement (§ 378 AO): refer. | Exact wording retained at current LF126, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF126 | - **Foreign partner in a German partnership.** A non-resident partner of a German commercial partnership has German income from a permanent establishment (§ 49(1) no. 2 (a) EStG): de-non-resident-tax. The determination is still made (§ 180(3) no. 1 AO excludes it only where just one partner is liable in Germany). | Exact wording retained at current LF130, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF127 | - **German partner in a foreign partnership.** Treaty-exempt profit is still determined where it matters for the partners' rate (§ 180(5) no. 1 AO) and raises the rate under § 32b(1) no. 3, with the EU/EEA exception for establishments that fail the active-business test. | Exact wording retained at current LF131, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF128 | - **Leaving Germany with a partnership share.** A partner's share is business property. Leaving the country can be a deemed withdrawal or a trigger under leaving-germany-exit-tax, which also names a limited partner with more than 25% of the income as having a substantial interest. Refer before the move. | Exact wording retained at current LF132, Cross-border points DE-R06: statutory tax/Soli withdrawal priority/exclusion and qualifying outside-liability exception preserved in summary; detailed-regime referral retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF129 | - **Arrival with foreign pay or rent.** Use Part 2. Foreign tax paid on income of the other period gives no credit in Germany, because Germany does not tax that income; it only raises the rate. | Exact wording retained at current LF133, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF130 | - **Social security.** Contributions linked to pay that a treaty exempts in Germany (the § 32b(1) no. 3 case) are not special expenses (§ 10(2) sentence 1 no. 1 EStG; BMF letter on Vorsorgeaufwendungen, Rn. 199), unless the pay was earned in an EU or EEA state or Switzerland and that state allows no deduction for them (§ 10(2) sentence 1 no. 1 (a) to (c)). Contributions on foreign pay of the months before arrival or after departure (the no. 2 case) are not settled here (see Not yet confirmed). Swiss AHV contributions do not reduce the amount added for the rate (H 32b). Social security itself: de-social-contributions. | Exact wording retained at current LF134, Cross-border points  |

Anna pays EUR 4,000 interest on a loan she took to buy her share (a Sonderbetriebsausgabe). No trade tax add-backs or reductions. Anna is single, no church, no other income, and her deductible insurance is EUR 15,000.
